# Graph Report - simplerest  (2026-11-27)

## Corpus Check
- 5480 files · ~24,443,760 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 59296 nodes · 110910 edges · 4918 communities (4113 shown, 805 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 89% EXTRACTED · 11% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 11840 edges (avg confidence: 0.67)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `08f0ff14`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
